Update total table arrays for wave function evaluation. 

Updates all table arrays required for wave function evaluation including winv, winvup, winvdo, tabpip, and related arrays. Handles electron-\/ion interactions, pseudopotentials, Jastrow factors, and periodic boundary conditions. Supports both VMC and DMC calculations with complex and real wave functions.
